CityTwin AI is an AI-powered Digital Twin that analyzes urban data, predicts future growth, and recommends optimal locations for hospitals, metro routes, schools, and other public infrastructure.
AI Component
Forecast infrastructure demand
Recommend optimal facility locations
Simulate multiple planning scenarios
Generate explainable AI recommendations
Innovation
AI + Digital Twin + GIS
Predict before building infrastructure
Virtual city scenario simulations
Future-ready urban decision support
